#==================================================
# Unique Buildings for Flavored Companies
#==================================================
#
# Every buildable entry carries ai_value = { value = te_company_flagship_ai_value }
# (common/script_values/te_company_ai_values.txt), after ai_nationalization_desire;
# test_company_ai.py fails on one without it.
#
# Every buildable entry allows one site per country: the state that already
# has the building, or any state of a country that has none. The two halves
# of the rule are shared triggers in te_company_triggers.txt:
#
#	potential = {
#		owner = { has_company = ... }
#		te_company_flagship_site_open = { BUILDING = X }
#	}
#	possible = {
#		te_company_flagship_one_site = { BUILDING = X }
#		te_company_flagship_builder_owns_state = yes
#	}
#	can_build_government = { te_company_flagship_builder_owns_state = yes }
#	can_build_private = { always = no }
#
# Only the state's owner builds or expands a flagship: a country with
# investment rights can't put up another country's flagship in its states.
# te_company_flagship_builder_owns_state (te_company_triggers.txt) compares the
# owner with scope:investor_country, which the engine saves during a
# construction check. Vanilla reads that scope from the Trade Center's
# can_build_*; it sits in possible as well so the rule holds on any path that
# checks only one of the two.
#
# `potential` uses `has_building` for the other states, an engine lookup that
# misses a site queued but not built, so the exact test, which sees one, sits
# in `possible`, vanilla's "can it be built now" check
# (game/common/buildings/buildings.md): the rule holds whenever anyone queues
# a site, and a second state shows the building greyed out with the tooltip
# instead of not at all. Both read the state's own site by the engine's
# `b:<type>` lookup, which sees a queued one, never a walk of the state's
# buildings: the weekly private investment pass reads a privately built
# type's `potential` and `possible` in every state (company buildings are a
# private group), up to some 300,000 times a week for one type
# (scripting_best_practices.md § "A Building's `possible` Runs in Every State
# for Private Investment, Before `can_build_private`").
#
# History (2026-10-03, late-game script profiles): the second branch was
# NOT = { any_state = { ... } }, scanned for every state of every owner on
# each ask; five of these blocks cost 10-14 ms a tick each. The engine
# documents any_state as global (one site in the whole world), while
# scripting_best_practices.md records it behaving owner-scoped from a
# building's potential; the owner chose one site per country, written out
# here. A first rewrite kept the exact scan in `potential` behind the
# has_building reject; the 32 basic-company buildings still cost 23.5 us a
# call (2.7 s in one capture) because most owners never build them, so the
# scan moved to `possible`. On 2026-11-22 the scans of each state's buildings
# became `b:` lookups (any_scope_building = { is_building_type = X } and
# exists = b:X find the same building) in the two shared triggers.

# --- Volkswagen ---
building_volkswagen_autostadt = {
	building_group = bg_company_buildings
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/company_icons/historical_company_icons/german_volkswagen.dds"
	expandable = yes
	downsizeable = yes
	has_max_level = yes
	unique = no
	city_type = city
	ai_nationalization_desire = 0
	ai_value = { value = te_company_flagship_ai_value }
	production_method_groups = {
		pmg_volkswagen_autostadt
	}
	required_construction = construction_cost_mega_high
	ownership_type = self
	can_build_government = {
		te_company_flagship_builder_owns_state = yes
	}
	can_build_private = {
		always = no
	}
	background = "gfx/interface/icons/building_icons/backgrounds/building_panel_bg_monuments.dds"
	potential = {
		owner = {
			has_company = company_type:company_volkswagen
		}
		te_company_flagship_site_open = { BUILDING = building_volkswagen_autostadt }
	}
	possible = {
		te_company_flagship_one_site = { BUILDING = building_volkswagen_autostadt }
		te_company_flagship_builder_owns_state = yes
	}
}
